Motivation: 从非语言视觉数据中理解社交互动对行为分析和活动监控至关重要，但现有方法多为端到端学习，缺乏可解释性，难以被非技术用户（如教师、照护者、社会工作者）理解和实际应用。

Method: 构建模块化框架：显式建模二元（成对）视觉注意力，基于心理学中的相互视觉注意理论，将头部朝向估计与轻量级几何推理相结合，汇聚生成个体和群体层面的可解释参与度指标。

Result: 在多种数据上通过定量实验验证了方法的有效性，并通过面向教师、照护者和社会工作者的定性可视化展示了其在理解群体互动动态方面的实际用途。

Conclusion: 该框架成功地将心理学理论与计算建模结合，在保持高准确性的同时提供可解释的输出，为社交互动分析提供了实用且易于理解的工具。

Abstract: Understanding social interactions from non-verbal visual data is important for behavior analysis and activity monitoring. We propose an interpretable computational model of social engagement inspired by psychological theories of mutual visual attention. Rather than learning interaction patterns end-to-end, our framework explicitly models dyadic visual attention and aggregates these cues into interpretable measures of individual and group engagement. The resulting modular framework combines state-of-the-art head orientation estimation with lightweight geometric reasoning, producing explanations that remain accessible to non-technical users. We evaluate the proposed approach on a variety of data through quantitative experiments and demonstrate its practical usefulness with qualitative visualizations designed to support teachers, caregivers, and social workers in understanding group interaction dynamics.

</details>


### [78] [Comparative Assessment of Deep Learning Architectures for Underwater Subsurface Kelp Forest Segmentation with The Kelp-o-Tron](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24594)
*Sundarabalan Balasubramanian,César Borja,Ana C. Murillo,Lexi N. Wilkes,Meredith L. McPherson,Kira A. Krumhansl,Jennifer A. Dijkstra,Jarrett E. K. Byrnes*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文系统比较了三种深度学习语义分割框架（ResNet34-U-Net、ResNet50-DeepLabV3和混合ResNet50-ASPP-Transformer）用于水下海带分割，基于美国东北部沿海采集的3,395张高分辨率RGB图像构建数据集。结果表明ResNet50-DeepLabV3（命名为Kelp-O-Tron）在Dice（0.7120）和IoU（0.6267）上表现最佳，兼顾了分割精度、鲁棒性和泛化能力，为自动化水下栖息地测绘和生态监测提供了资源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 水下海带森林是重要的沿海生态系统，但水下海带分割面临光学退化、光照变化、水体浑浊、植被重叠和复杂底质背景等挑战，现有方法难以实现准确分割，亟需系统评估先进的深度学习分割框架以提高自动化监测能力。

Method: 构建了3,395个SSeg辅助标注的图像-掩膜对数据集，采用一致的数据预处理、增强和评估协议，系统评估三种深度学习分割框架：ResNet34-U-Net、ResNet50-DeepLabV3及混合ResNet50-ASPP-Transformer，并使用地理上独立的站点数据进行定量和定性评估。

Result: ResNet50-DeepLabV3在独立测试数据上取得最高Dice（0.7120）和IoU（0.6267）；ResNet34-U-Net次之（Dice 0.6868；IoU 0.5978）；混合ASPP-Transformer获得最高像素精度（0.8528）但Dice（0.6437）和IoU（0.5746）较低。外部定性评估显示DeepLabV3在不同环境条件下的分割一致性最佳。

Conclusion: ResNet50-DeepLabV3（Kelp-O-Tron）在分割精度、鲁棒性和泛化能力上取得了最佳平衡，可作为水下海带自动化分割的优选方案。所提供的数据集、标注流程和比较评估为推进自动化水下栖息地测绘和生态监测奠定了基础。

Abstract: Submerged kelp forests are vital coastal ecosystems that support marine biodiversity and ecosystem dynamics, yet accurate underwater kelp segmentation remains challenging due to optical degradation, illumination variability, turbidity, overlapping vegetation, and complex benthic backgrounds. We systematically evaluated three deep learning semantic segmentation frameworks, ResNet34-U-Net, ResNet50-DeepLabV3, and a hybrid ResNet50-ASPP-Transformer architecture, for kelp detection using high-resolution underwater RGB imagery collected from northeastern U.S. coastal waters. A dataset of 3,395 SSeg assisted annotated image-mask pairs was developed for model training and validation, while geographically independent sites were used for quantitative and qualitative evaluation. All models used consistent preprocessing, augmentation, and evaluation protocols. On independent test data, ResNet50-DeepLabV3 achieved the highest Dice (0.7120) and Intersection over Union (IoU; 0.6267), followed by ResNet34 U Net (Dice 0.6868; IoU 0.5978). The hybrid ASPP Transformer achieved the highest pixel accuracy (0.8528) but lower Dice (0.6437) and IoU (0.5746). External qualitative evaluation further showed that DeepLabV3 produced more consistent segmentation across varying environmental conditions, image qualities, and benthic habitats. Overall, ResNet50-DeepLabV3, termed Kelp-O-Tron, provided the best balance of segmentation accuracy, robustness, and generalization. The dataset, annotation workflow, and comparative evaluation provide resources for advancing automated underwater habitat mapping and ecological monitoring.

</details>


### [79] [Towards Reliable AI-Based Histological Staining: A Systematic Study of Scaling and Uncertainty in Unpaired Generative Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24626)
*Qasim Siddiqui,Adrian Friebel,Maiju Myllys,Zaynab Hobloss,Daniela Gonzalez,Ahmed Ghallab,Stefan Hoehme*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文首次系统性地对六种无监督图像转换架构（基于GAN和基于扩散模型）在H&E到天狼星红（SR）虚拟染色任务上进行了基准测试，涵盖54种缩放配置，并首次对无监督染色转换架构的认知不确定性进行了深度集成比较。研究发现感知质量、任务错误和集成一致性衡量的是模型适应性的独立轴，单一指标无法全面评估模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 肝纤维化分期依赖天狼星红（SR）染色进行胶原蛋白定量评估，但SR染色并非所有临床中心都能开展，且消耗组织、时间和试剂成本。虽然基于AI的虚拟染色可以从H&E生成SR图像，但缺乏系统性的无监督模型基准测试，且预测不确定性未被量化——视觉上合理的输出可能无法忠实再现底层组织结构。

Method: 在全新发布的配对H&E到SR小鼠肝脏数据集上，对六个无监督图像到图像转换架构（含GAN和扩散模型）在54种缩放配置下进行基准测试。每个配置从感知、分布和任务特异性三个轴以及盲法专家阅读研究进行评估；每类最优模型再训练为深度集成，进行无监督染色转换架构间认知不确定性的首次系统比较。

Result: 研究发现感知质量、任务特异性错误和集成一致性衡量的是基本独立的模型适应性轴：GAN类方法在感知指标上紧密聚集但在任务错误和集成一致性上差异显著，而扩散方法（CycleDiffusion）在所有三个轴上均表现不同。没有任何单一指标能捕获这些差异。

Conclusion: 可靠的虚拟染色需要在三个轴上联合报告和选择模型，而非依赖单一指标。数据集、切片流水线、模型和评估代码均已公开发布，为这一翻译任务提供了首个开源资源。

Abstract: Liver fibrosis, the principal predictor of long-term outcome in chronic liver disease, is staged from histological estimates of collagen content. Sirius Red (SR) provides the standard quantitative readout (collagen proportionate area, CPA) but is not acquired at every clinical centre and consumes tissue, time, and reagent cost beyond the routine Hematoxylin and eosin (H&E) stain. AI-based virtual staining can generate SR directly from H&E, yet systematic benchmarks of unsupervised models are scarce and their predictive uncertainty has not been quantified, even though visually plausible outputs may not faithfully reproduce the underlying tissue structure. We therefore benchmark six unsupervised image-to-image architectures (GAN-based and diffusion-based) across 54 scaling configurations on a newly released paired H&E to SR mouse liver dataset, the first open resource for this translation task. Each configuration is evaluated jointly on perceptual, distributional, and task-specific axes plus a blinded expert reader study; the best per family is then retrained as a deep ensemble, the first systematic comparison of epistemic uncertainty across unsupervised stain-to-stain architectures. Across families, perceptual quality, task-specific error, and ensemble agreement measure largely independent axes of model fitness: GAN-based methods cluster tightly on perceptual metrics yet differ substantially on task error and ensemble agreement, while the diffusion-based method (CycleDiffusion) is qualitatively different on all three. No single metric captures these differences, so reliable virtual staining requires reporting and selecting on all three jointly. The dataset, tiling pipeline, models, and evaluation code are released publicly.

</details>


### [80] [On-Policy Self-Distillation in Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24646)
*Wei Zhou,Xiongwei Zhu,Lingdong Kong,Bo Chen,Lei Zhang,Yongyuan Liang,Xiaoxia Hou,Ye Tian,Xian Sun,Yingshuo Wang,Linfeng Li,Shengqiong Wu,Leigang Qu,Feng Li,Wei Liu,Julian McAuley,Tat-Seng Chua*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出DiffusionOPSD，一个基于策略的自我蒸馏框架，将图像级奖励信号转化为扩散模型去噪过程中显式的中间监督目标，通过冻结行为策略生成轨迹和锚点，训练策略拟合构造的正负目标，并利用指数移动平均更新行为策略，在多个评估器上取得最优成绩且显著降低训练成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习可以将扩散模型与人类偏好对齐，但端点奖励无法指定中间去噪预测应如何变化，导致中间监督缺失、训练效率低下且难以分析。

Method: 采用on-policy自我蒸馏框架：冻结行为策略生成轨迹提供查询状态和锚点；奖励梯度在锚点周围构造有界正负目标；可训练策略通过有限次拟合这些目标作为分离监督；指数移动平均更新刷新行为策略。

Result: 在SD 3.5-M和Z-Image-Turbo两个骨干网络上、十个评估器的20个奖励匹配设置中取得19个最佳分数，比最强对比方法最高提升44.0%，训练GPU小时比DiffusionNFT减少40%（SD 3.5-M）和63%（Z-Image-Turbo）。

Conclusion: 验证了on-policy自我蒸馏是将图像级奖励引导转换为显式且持续刷新的中间监督的高效且可分析方法，为更高效和可诊断的扩散模型对齐开辟了路径。同时发现目标构造收益与单次拟合后的实际收益不一定成正比。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning can align diffusion models with human preferences and task-specific objectives, but endpoint rewards do not specify how an intermediate denoising prediction should change. We introduce DiffusionOPSD as an on-policy self-distillation framework that converts image-level reward guidance into explicit targets for clean-output predictions at sampled queries. At each outer iteration, a frozen behavior policy generates trajectories and supplies query states and anchors. Reward gradients construct bounded positive and negative targets around each anchor. The trainable policy fits these targets as detached supervision through finite fitting before an exponential moving average update refreshes the behavior policy. This setup lets us measure target construction and finite realization separately. Controlled same-query experiments show that larger target-construction gains do not necessarily translate into larger realized gains after a single fitting update. Across SD 3.5-M and the step-distilled Z-Image-Turbo, our approach achieves the best final held-out scores in 19 of 20 reward-matched settings across two backbones and ten evaluators. It outperforms the strongest competing method by up to 44.0% and reduces training GPU-hours relative to DiffusionNFT by 40% on SD 3.5-M and 63% on Z-Image-Turbo. These results support on-policy self-distillation as an efficient and analyzable approach to diffusion post-training by converting image-level reward guidance into explicit and continually refreshed intermediate supervision, thereby opening a path toward more efficient and diagnosable alignment.

</details>


### [81] [ReGround-Surg: Reliability-Guided Anchor Grounding for Referring Surgical Video Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24671)
*Jiaxin Wen,Ming Yin,Lu Liu,Zeyu Fu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出ReGround-Surg框架，通过预测文本条件空间可靠性图来改进SAM2-based手术视频指代分割，解决初始锚定掩码质量不佳导致误差传播的问题，在Ref-EndoVis17和Ref-EndoVis18数据集上取得一致提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SAM2-based两阶段方法（如ReSurgSAM2）先grounding目标再通过tracking传播掩码，但其性能高度依赖初始grounding掩码的质量。一旦初始锚定错误，后续追踪会不断传播误差。尤其在手术视频中，因仪器外观相似、遮挡及组织-器械复杂交互，该问题尤为突出。

Method: 提出ReGround-Surg轻量级可靠性引导锚定框架。核心是预测文本条件空间可靠性图，并复用于两个互补分支：(1) Gated Side Adapter增强与表达式相关的视觉区域后再进行文本-视觉融合；(2) Reliability-Weighted Vision-to-Text Attention在提示token聚合时抑制偏离目标的视觉证据。

Result: 在Ref-EndoVis17和Ref-EndoVis18两个数据集上，跨三个评估划分均取得优于现有最先进方法的一致改进，且速度几乎无损失。

Conclusion: ReGround-Surg通过可靠性图引导的锚定机制有效缓解了初始掩码质量敏感性问题，为手术视频指代分割提供了一种轻量且高效的改进方案。

Abstract: Referring surgical video segmentation requires segmenting a target instrument or tissue region across video frames according to a natural language expression. Recent Segment Anything Model 2 (SAM2) based two-stage methods (e.g., ReSurgSAM2) first ground the referred target in an initial or selected frame, then propagate the selected mask via tracking. Although effective, their performance is highly sensitive to the quality of the initial grounded mask: once an incorrect anchor is selected, subsequent tracking tends to propagate the error. This issue is especially challenging in surgical videos due to visually similar instruments, occlusion, and complex tissue-tool interactions. To address this issue, we propose ReGround-Surg, a lightweight reliability-guided anchor grounding framework to improve SAM2-based referring surgical video segmentation. It first predicts a text-conditioned spatial reliability map from the referring expression and current-frame visual features. The map is then reused in two complementary branches: a Gated Side Adapter enhances expression-relevant visual regions before text-to-vision fusion, while a Reliability-Weighted Vision-to-Text Attention module suppresses off-target visual evidence during prompt-token aggregation. Experiments on Ref-EndoVis17 and Ref-EndoVis18 show consistent improvements over state-of-the-art methods across three evaluation splits with negligible speed reduction. Code is publicly available at https://github.com/JiaxinWen1/ReGround-Surg.

</details>


### [82] [TurboT2VA: Fast Large-Scale Text-to-Video-Audio Generation via Score-Regularized Consistency Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24674)
*Xiaoda Yang,Yuxiang Liu,Kaiwen Zheng,Yuan Liu,Yibo Lai,Shengpeng Ji,Kai Jiang,Jianfei Chen,Xiaobin Hu,Shuicheng Yan,Jintao Zhang,Jun Zhu,Zhou Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出TurboT2VA，一个用于加速19B参数联合文生视频-音频（T2VA）模型的蒸馏与推理框架。通过逐模态归一化和渐进式课程学习（离散一致性预热、连续一致性精炼、联合一致性-分布匹配），在LTX-2上将生成器延迟从50.52s降至2.51s（20.1倍加速），结合架构感知推理栈（W8A8量化、融合算子、文本填充压缩、模态感知稀疏注意力），在高分辨率1024×1792下实现318.74s到5.83s的54.67倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联合文生视频-音频生成需要产生同步的视觉和声学内容，但大模型的长时间采样轨迹和异构多模态计算使得推理成本极其高昂，亟需高效的蒸馏与推理加速方案。

Method: 提出TurboT2VA框架：(1) 逐模态归一化解决模态不平衡优化问题；(2) 渐进式课程学习，依次包含离散一致性预热、连续一致性精炼、联合一致性-分布匹配三个阶段，先建立稳定多样的生成轨迹再引入分布级精炼；(3) 架构感知推理栈，结合受保护的W8A8量化、融合算子、填充文本压缩以及模态感知稀疏注意力，同时保留稠密的跨模态和文本条件路径。

Result: 在LTX-2上，四步蒸馏使生成器延迟从50.52s降至2.51s（512×768标准分辨率，20.1倍加速），保持强视觉质量、音频保真度、多样性和视音频同步。高分辨率1024×1792部署下，完整堆栈将生成器延迟从318.74s降至5.83s（单块NVIDIA H20，54.67倍生成器加速）。

Conclusion: TurboT2VA通过针对大规模T2VA蒸馏的模态不平衡与质量-多样性权衡等挑战，结合蒸馏课程与架构感知推理优化，实现了显著的推理加速效果，同时保持了生成质量和模态同步性。

Abstract: Joint text-to-video-audio generation produces synchronized visual and acoustic content, but the long sampling trajectories and heterogeneous multimodal computation of large models make inference prohibitively expensive. We present TurboT2VA, a distillation and inference framework for accelerating a 19B-parameter joint video-audio model. Large-scale T2VA distillation is challenged by modality-imbalanced optimization, the difficulty of continuous-time consistency training at scale, and the quality--diversity trade-off. TurboT2VA addresses these issues with per-modality normalization and a progressive curriculum comprising discrete consistency warm-up, continuous consistency refinement, and joint consistency--distribution matching. The curriculum first establishes a stable, diverse generation trajectory and only then introduces distribution-level refinement. On LTX-2, four-step distillation reduces generator latency from 50.52s to 2.51s at the standard evaluation resolution of 512$\times$768, achieving a 20.1$\times$ speedup while maintaining strong visual quality, audio fidelity, diversity, and video-audio synchronization. We further develop an architecture-aware inference stack that combines guarded W8A8 and fused operators, padded-text compaction, and modality-aware sparse attention while preserving dense cross-modal and text-conditioning paths. Under the high-resolution deployment setting at 1024$\times$1792, the complete stack reduces generator latency from 318.74s to 5.83s on one NVIDIA H20, achieving a 54.67$\times$ generator-only speedup. Inference code and generation demos are available at https://github.com/thu-ml/TurboDiffusion/tree/main/turbot2va.

</details>


### [83] [Game2World Engine: Unlocking In-the-Wild Gameplay Videos for World Model Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24680)
*Wenxuan Shen,Dongna Jin,Dongping Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出GameUI-Taxonomy和G2WEngine全栈框架，用于从游戏视频中自动化提取UI资产、合成时间一致的UI叠加层，并构建了包含96K合成配对视频和1,079个野外片段的Game2World数据集。基于此提出GameCleaner，一种无需掩码的游戏UI去除模型，能直接识别并移除各类HUD元素。实验表明，基于无UI游戏视频训练的世界模型在VideoReward上提升6.83%，GameCleaner在合成视频上AAR达95.36，野外视频AAR达80.05。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原始游戏视频中屏幕空间界面（HUD/UI）与游戏世界纠缠在一起，引入游戏特有偏差和无关动态，阻碍视频世界模型的训练。现有方法依赖掩码或无法有效处理UI去除问题。

Method: 构建了三个核心组件：1) GameUI-Taxonomy——将UI元素划分为21个分类的体系；2) G2WEngine——自动提取可复用UI资产并合成时间一致的UI叠加层；3) GameCleaner——结合多模态语义理解与视频编辑能力的免掩码UI去除模型。

Result: 世界模型在无UI数据上训练比有UI数据训练VideoReward提升6.83%；GameCleaner在合成视频上AAR达95.36，超越最强时间掩码基线57.3%；野外视频AAR为80.05，背景保留率达99.8%。数据集包含来自303款游戏的96K合成视频和1,079个野外片段，资产库含5,132个验证过的UI元素。

Conclusion: 通过系统化UI去除，能够将海量互联网游戏视频转化为高质量世界模型训练数据，展现出扩展这一范式的巨大潜力。

Abstract: Video games provide a scalable source of training data for video world models, offering diverse environments, complex interactions, and abundant in-the-wild gameplay videos. However, raw gameplay footage entangles the game world with screen-space interfaces, introducing game-specific biases and irrelevant dynamics that hinder world-model training. To address this problem, we introduce GameUI-Taxonomy and G2WEngine, a full-stack framework that formalizes gameplay UI grounding and removal. G2WEngine automatically extracts reusable UI assets from real gameplay videos and synthesizes temporally coherent UI overlays on clean footage. Using this engine, we construct Game2World, comprising 96K synthetic paired videos with precise reconstruction targets and 1,079 in-the-wild clips from 303 games for realistic evaluation. Its asset library contains 5,132 verified UI elements across 21 taxonomy categories, collected from 1,010 representative gameplay frames. Based on Game2World, we propose GameCleaner, a mask-free gameplay UI removal model that combines multimodal semantic understanding with video editing capabilities. Unlike mask-based methods, GameCleaner directly identifies and removes diverse HUD elements while preserving the underlying scene content and temporal dynamics. In a controlled pilot, world models trained on UI-free gameplay improve overall VideoReward by 6.83% over those trained on UI-overlaid data. On UI-removal evaluation, GameCleaner achieves an average AAR of 95.36 on synthetic videos, outperforming the strongest temporal mask baseline by 57.3%, and obtains the best in-the-wild AAR of 80.05 with 99.8 background preservation. These results demonstrate the scalable potential of transforming Internet gameplay videos into high-quality world-model training data. Code, dataset, and model will be available at https://github.com/Dongping-Chen/Game2World.

</details>


### [84] [Deep Learning Super Resolution for Satellite Cloud Mask Downscaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24715)
*Angelos Georgakis,Valentina Kanaki,Giorgos Giannopoulos,Stella Girtsou,Ioannis Kontogiorgakis,Charalampos Kontoes,Kostas Philippopoulos*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出两种深度学习超分辨率方法（SpatialCNN和SpatialGAN），用于将SEVIRI云掩膜产品降尺度到MODIS分辨率（4倍空间增强），并创建了跨传感器云掩膜数据集SEVMOD-CM，实验验证了该方法优于双三次插值，对大气监测、天气预报等应用具有价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大量光学卫星数据受云雾影响，且卫星数据存在空间与时间分辨率之间的固有权衡，难以获取连续高分辨率的云观测数据。现有方法无法有效解决这一问题，亟需新的技术手段实现云掩膜产品的高精度降尺度。

Method: 构建了SEVMOD-CM跨传感器云掩膜数据集（通过空间和时间匹配MODIS与SEVIRI观测数据），并提出了两种深度学习方法：基于CNN的SpatialCNN和基于GAN的SpatialGAN。模型以SEVIRI光谱和云掩膜产品为输入，预测对应的MODIS云掩膜，实现4倍空间分辨率增强，并与双三次插值上采样方法进行对比评估。

Result: 实验结果表明，提出的SpatialCNN和SpatialGAN方法在云掩膜降尺度任务上优于标准双三次插值方法，成功实现了跨传感器域的4倍空间增强，验证了所提出模型和数据集在遥感领域的有效性。

Conclusion: 该研究证明了将超分辨率技术应用于地球静止卫星云掩膜产品的显著效益，为大气监测、天气预报、灾害风险降低、太阳能预报和气候研究等应用提供了有价值的技术支撑和数据集资源。

Abstract: A vast amount of optical satellite data is being transmitted to Earth-based servers every day, and more than half of this data is affected by haze or clouds. Additionally, this data suffers from the fundamental trade-off between spatial and temporal resolution, which remains largely unresolved, making the acquisition of continuous high-resolution satellite observations of clouds an ongoing challenge. This work addresses this challenge by proposing two Deep Learning super-resolution methods for the accurate downscaling of SEVIRI cloud mask products, as well as a novel cross-sensor cloud mask dataset called SEVMOD-CM, created by spatially and temporally matching MODIS and SEVIRI satellite observations. The two proposed models are a CNN-based (SpatialCNN) and a GAN-based (SpatialGAN) Neural Network. Trained on the SEVIRI spectral and cloud mask products, the proposed methods predict the corresponding MODIS Cloud masks, achieving a 4x spatial enhancement across sensor domains. Both approaches are evaluated experimentally, and compared against the standard bicubic interpolation upsampling technique. The experimental results demonstrate the value of the proposed models and dataset for the remote sensing community, highlighting the benefits of applying super-resolution techniques to geostationary-derived cloud mask products for applications such as atmospheric monitoring, weather forecasting, disaster risk reduction, solar energy forecasting, and climate research.

</details>


### [85] [Interpretable Fundus Image Classification via Ring-Based Retinal Vasculature Features](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24723)
*Xiaoyan Li,Shixin Xu,Arvind Gupta,Huaxiong Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于视盘为中心的环形视网膜血管结构表示的可解释性眼底图像分类框架，仅使用定量血管描述符（几何、颜色、氧合相关外观和熵）即可在三个公共眼底数据集上取得强分类性能，在HRF数据集上达到91.1%的准确率，与预训练视觉Transformer模型RETFound相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代眼底图像分类管线依赖深度潜在表示，缺乏可解释性，限制了其临床应用价值和对疾病机制的深入理解。

Method: 以视盘为中心的同心环结构表示视网膜血管网络，在环内聚合血管几何、颜色外观、氧合相关血管外观及血管-背景熵等生理学驱动的描述符，这些描述符基于血管掩膜、图像强度和光密度测量导出。

Result: 在HRF数据集上使用自动生成的血管掩膜达到91.1%准确率，与在大规模视网膜眼底数据上预训练的视觉Transformer RETFound在相同评估设置下表现相当。额外分析发现预训练图像模型对采集相关的空间线索（如眼底尺度和视野内视网膜位置）及非血管图像特征敏感。

Conclusion: 该框架无需大规模任务特定训练数据即可支持可解释的疾病分类、定量视网膜表型分析和视网膜生物标志物发现。

Abstract: Retinal fundus photography is widely used for screening and monitoring ocular diseases, but many modern classification pipelines rely on deep latent representations and provide limited interpretability. This study develops an interpretable fundus image classification framework based on a ring-structured representation of the retinal vasculature centered on the optic disc. The method quantifies vessel geometry, color appearance, oxygenation-related vascular appearance, and vessel--background entropy within concentric retinal regions. These physiologically motivated descriptors are derived from vessel masks, image intensities, and optical-density measurements and aggregated across rings to capture spatial variation in vascular properties. Using only quantitative vascular descriptors, the proposed method achieved strong classification performance across three public fundus datasets. On HRF, it achieved 91.1\% accuracy using automatically generated vessel masks, matching RETFound, a vision transformer pretrained on large-scale retinal fundus image data, under the same evaluation setting. Additional analyses suggest that pretrained image models are sensitive to acquisition-related spatial cues, including fundus scale and retinal position within the field of view, as well as broader non-vessel image characteristics. This framework may support interpretable disease classification, quantitative retinal phenotyping, and retinal biomarker discovery without requiring large task-specific training datasets.

</details>


### [86] [TorchMorph: CUDA-accelerated Morphological Transforms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24738)
*Kai Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TorchMorph是一个轻量级PyTorch扩展，将scipy.ndimage的形态学算子移植到GPU上，提供22个公共算子（二值/灰度形态学、距离变换、最优传输），支持最多8维CUDA张量，API与scipy.ndimage完全兼容，性能最高提升1100倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: scipy.ndimage作为Python生态中形态学变换的事实标准实现，仅支持CPU和单数组处理，无法直接在GPU训练循环中使用（需要昂贵的设备-主机往返传输）。现有的PyTorch GPU视觉库覆盖的算子子集很窄，通常限制在二维空间和扁平结构元素，存在明显的功能缺口。

Method: TorchMorph采用分层架构，所有算子实现为融合CUDA内核，直接操作(B, C, Spatial...)维度（支持最多8个空间维度）的CUDA张量。API设计完全镜像scipy.ndimage的参数签名，包括边界模式、结构元素原点和预分配输出。算子族涵盖二值形态学、灰度形态学、精确/近似距离变换以及熵正则化最优传输（Sinkhorn求解器）。

Result: 批处理执行相比单线程CPU参考，灰度形态学吞吐量最高提升1100倍，精确欧氏距离变换最高提升350倍；Sinkhorn求解器比POT快42倍。二值和chamfer算子与SciPy对应实现完全一致，所有浮点算子与CPU参考的绝对误差小于1.8e-6。

Conclusion: TorchMorph填补了PyTorch生态中GPU形态学算子的空白，以MIT许可证开源发布，通过保持与scipy.ndimage的参数级兼容性，使得现有流程只需更改导入即可迁移到GPU，同时提供显著的速度提升。

Abstract: Morphological transforms are long-standing tools for shape and mask processing, but the de facto reference implementation in the Python ecosystem, i.e. scipy.ndimage, is CPU-only, single-array, and therefore unusable inside a GPU training loop without an expensive device-to-host round trip. GPU vision libraries built on PyTorch cover a narrow subset of these operators, typically restricted to two spatial dimensions and flat structuring elements. We present TorchMorph, a lightweight PyTorch extension that closes this gap. TorchMorph exposes 22 public operators covering binary morphology, greyscale morphology, exact and approximate distance transforms, and entropy-regularised optimal transport, all implemented as fused CUDA kernels that operate directly on (B, C, Spatial...) CUDA tensors with up to eight spatial dimensions. The API deliberately mirrors scipy.ndimage argument-for-argument, including border modes, structuring-element origins and pre-allocated outputs, so that existing pipelines port with a change of import. We describe the layered architecture and the kernel designs behind each operator family. Against single-threaded CPU references, batched execution reaches up to 1.1e3 times the throughput of scipy.ndimage on greyscale morphology and up to 350x on exact Euclidean distance transforms, while the Sinkhorn solver runs up to 42x faster than POT. Binary and chamfer operators reproduce their SciPy counterparts exactly, and every float-valued operator agrees with the CPU reference to within 1.8e-6 absolute error. TorchMorph is released under the MIT licence at https://intcomp.github.io/tm.

</details>


### [87] [Weakly Supervised Seafloor Segmentation for Seagrass Habitat Mapping in Side-Scan Sonar Imagery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24756)
*Hayat Rajani,Nuno Gracias,Rafael Garcia*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种弱监督语义分割框架，仅利用图像级标签即可从侧扫声纳（SSS）影像中实现海草床底栖栖息地的高精度像素级制图，在保留集上达到87.6%的mIoU，自监督预训练进一步提升3%，为海岸尺度的海草监测提供了标签高效的技术方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 海草床是重要的蓝碳栖息地，其制图对海岸管理和碳清单至关重要。光学卫星无法覆盖深水或浑浊水域，而侧扫声纳虽可在任意深度高分辨率成像，但其解译严重依赖密集的人工标注，成本高且速度慢，亟需标签高效的自动化方法。

Method: 将弱监督语义分割框架适配到SSS底栖栖息地制图：1）采用ViT编码器-解码器与分类分支耦合，提取类别激活图（CAM）；2）利用针对声学影像噪声和弱边界调优的稠密条件随机场（CRF）将CAM细化为伪标签；3）采用迭代自训练方案，并结合采样策略应对类别不平衡；4）评估不同损失函数，发现Lovász-Softmax损失最有效；5）使用无标注SSS数据进行自监督预训练进一步提升性能。

Result: 在保留测试剖面上，细化后的伪标签对真值达到89.3%的mIoU；仅用图像级标签训练的分割分支达到87.6%的mIoU；自监督预训练额外提升3%的mIoU。野外试验进一步验证了模型的可泛化性。

Conclusion: 研究证明，从侧扫声纳进行准确且标签高效的底栖栖息地制图是可行的，可满足海岸尺度海草监测的需求，为大规模蓝碳栖息地调查提供了实用的技术路径。

Abstract: Seagrass meadows are crucial blue-carbon habitats, and mapping their extent is a prerequisite for coastal management and carbon inventory. Optical satellite sensors cover large areas but cannot reach deep or turbid water, whereas side-scan sonar (SSS) images the seabed at high resolution and at any depth. Interpreting SSS, however, still relies on dense manual annotation, which is slow and costly. We address this by adapting a weakly supervised semantic segmentation framework to SSS benthic habitat mapping, so that pixel-level maps are learned from image-level labels alone. The framework couples a ViT-based encoder-decoder with a classification branch, extracts class activation maps, and refines them into pseudo-labels with a dense conditional random field that we tune for the noise and weak boundaries of acoustic imagery. It follows an iterative self-training scheme, together with a sampling strategy to cope with the strong class imbalance of the data. We also study the effect of different loss functions on segmentation quality, finding Lovász-Softmax loss the most effective. On a held-out transect, the refined pseudo-labels reached an mIoU of 89.3\% against the ground truth, and the segmentation branch, trained without any pixel-level labels, reached 87.6\%. Self-supervised pretraining on unlabelled SSS added a further 3\% in mean intersection-over-union. Field trials further demonstrate the generalizability of the trained model. These results show that accurate and label-efficient benthic habitat mapping from side-scan sonar is feasible at the scale needed for coast-wide seagrass monitoring.

</details>


### [88] [DiD It in 87 Minutes: A Label-Free Softmax-to-Linear Adaptation of Vision Transformers for Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22368)
*Huaiyuan Qin,Gabriel James Goenawan,Zihang Lin,Muli Yang,Hongyuan Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出检测器接口蒸馏（DiD）方法，通过对齐检测器面对的张量而非内部隐藏状态，将预训练的Softmax注意力检测器骨干网络转换为线性注意力版本，无需标签即可实现高效转换。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 线性注意力在高分辨率目标检测中具有全局token混合的低成本优势，但将Softmax注意力ViT骨干直接替换为线性注意力会导致严重性能下降。现有的标签自由蒸馏方法对分类任务有效，却往往在检测任务上失效，核心挑战在于保持检测器接口：转换后的骨干必须精确再现固定下游检测器期望的特征张量，而不仅仅是模仿内部的Softmax隐藏状态。

Method: 提出检测器接口蒸馏（DiD）方法，这是一种无标签转换方法，仅训练线性注意力骨干网络，通过将检测器面对面的接口张量与冻结的Softmax教师对齐来实现转换。

Result: 在DOTA-v1.5数据集上，DiD大幅优于已有基线方法，且性能与监督式全训练线性模型相当。适配过程在4块GPU上约87分钟完成，线性化骨干网络将推理延迟降低约62%，峰值内存减少约49%。

Conclusion: DiD为社区提供了一条简单、无标签的路径，可将已训练的Softmax检测器复用为高效的线性检测器，并鼓励未来架构转换工作中采用接口感知的优化目标。

Abstract: While linear attention is a compelling mechanism for high-resolution object detection due to its reduced cost for global token mixing, converting the Softmax-attention ViT backbone of a trained detector into a linear-attention one is not a trivial drop-in replacement. Directly swapping the attention operator leads to severe performance degradation, and generic label-free distillation, though effective for classification, often fails on detection tasks. We argue that the central challenge is \textit{detector-interface preservation}: the converted backbone must reproduce the exact feature tensors expected by the fixed downstream detector, rather than merely imitating internal Softmax hidden states. To address this, we introduce Detector-Interface Distillation (DiD), a label-free conversion method that exclusively trains the linear-attention backbone by aligning detector-facing interface tensors with those of a frozen Softmax teacher. On DOTA-v1.5, DiD substantially outperforms established baselines and matches supervised, fully trained linear models. Adaptation completes in roughly 87 minutes on 4 GPUs, and the linearized backbone cuts inference latency by ~62% and peak memory by ~49%. We hope our findings offer the community a simple, label-free route to reusing trained Softmax detectors as efficient linear ones, and encourage interface-aware objectives in future architecture-conversion work.

</details>


### [89] [IDeaL: Data-Free Multi-Teacher Distillation via Improved Dead Leaves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24759)
*Feyza Yavuz,Mert Bülent Sarıyıldız,Diane Larlus*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无需原始训练数据（data-free）的多教师蒸馏方法。作者首先研究了向教师模型输入不同类型噪声时蒸馏效果的上限，然后利用教师自身包含的信息，通过补丁级（patch）和图像级（image）的去相关损失来生成针对每个教师优化的改进样本，用于数据无关的多教师蒸馏。实验表明，该方法生成的样本（IDeaL）能使学生在捕捉教师互补信息方面表现优异，显著缩小了与使用真实图像蒸馏的差距；在1K图像蒸馏预算下，使用IDeaL样本蒸馏的学生甚至匹配或超越了使用ImageNet 1K子集蒸馏的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统多教师蒸馏假设可以获取教师训练数据的并集来训练学生模型，但这一假设在许多实际场景中并不成立。本文质疑该假设，探索在无法获取原始训练数据的情况下，如何有效进行多教师蒸馏，即研究基于噪声输入的数据无关蒸馏方案。

Method: 首先评估使用不同类型噪声输入蒸馏教师模型的性能上限；然后提出利用教师模型自身包含的信息来定制优化噪声：通过补丁级和图像级的去相关损失，生成针对特定教师优化的改进样本（称为IDeaL），从而在无需真实数据的情况下提升多教师蒸馏效果。

Result: 实验表明，使用IDeaL样本训练的学生能够成功捕捉教师的互补信息，性能表现令人惊喜，显著缩小了与使用真实图像蒸馏的学生之间的差距。在1K图像的蒸馏预算下，使用IDeaL样本蒸馏的学生匹配或超越了使用ImageNet 1K子集蒸馏的学生表现。

Conclusion: 本文证明即使没有原始训练数据，通过精心设计的教师特定噪声样本（IDeaL），多教师蒸馏仍然可以实现接近甚至媲美真实数据蒸馏的效果，为数据无关的知识蒸馏提供了新的有效途径。

Abstract: Multi-teacher distillation has emerged as a way to combine complementary teacher models into a single student model that exhibits the strengths of all its teachers. The student is trained to mimic the output of the teachers on a set of images, typically the union of the individual teacher's training sets, assuming this data is available. In this paper, we question that assumption and explore alternative options. We first study how far one can go when distilling from teachers fed with different types of noise. Then, we show that information contained in the teachers can be leveraged to tailor the noise for multi-teacher distillation: we propose a method that, thanks to decorrelation losses at both patch and image levels, generates teacher-specific, improved samples optimized for data-free distillation. Experiments show that our most effective samples, IDeaL, lead to strong students that successfully capture complementary information from the teachers, yielding surprisingly competitive results that substantially narrow the gap with students distilled from real images. Moreover, given a limited budget of 1K images for distillation, students distilled using our IDeaL samples match or surpass the performance of those distilled using a 1K-image subset of ImageNet.

</details>


### [90] [MoTE: Mixture of Task Experts for Multi-Task Video Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24763)
*Muhammad Asad Ali,Umar Khan,Nadia Robertini,Didier Stricker*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出MoTE（Mixture of Task Experts）解码器架构，将大语言模型的前馈网络转换为任务专属专家，同时保持多模态骨干网络共享。每个样本遵循一条样本级任务路由，使活跃任务专家计算量独立于存储的专家数量。在五个COIN基准上，五专家模型每样本激活约20亿LLM参数，平均top-1准确率优于近期VideoLLM基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 程序性视频语言模型需要从相同的视觉证据中解决异质任务（动作识别、预测、程序预测）。现有密集Transformer解码器跨任务共享前馈网络，导致任务行为纠缠、能力扩展困难；而稀疏MoE解码器的token级学习路由与任务级程序性目标自然不一致。

Method: 提出MoTE解码器架构，将LLM前馈网络转换为任务专属专家（task-specific experts），同时保持多模态骨干共享。每个示例遵循一条样本级任务路由（sample-level task route），使得活跃的任务专家计算量不随存储专家数量增加而增长。具体实例化为VideoLLM-MoTE模型，使用显式任务路由。

Result: 在五个COIN基准上，五专家模型每样本仅激活约20亿LLM参数，平均top-1准确率高于近期VideoLLM基线。在相同的专家拓扑下，该方法优于密集全专家激活方案和学习式稀疏路由控制方案。

Conclusion: 任务结构化路由为多任务视频语言学习提供了一种可解释且计算高效的解码器替代方案，证明了任务级路由相比token级路由和学习式稀疏控制在多任务场景下具有明显优势。

Abstract: Procedural video-language models must solve heterogeneous tasks from the same visual evidence, including action recognition, forecasting, and procedure prediction. Dense transformer decoders share the same feed-forward networks across tasks, which can entangle task behavior and make controlled capability expansion difficult. Sparse Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) decoders provide conditional computation, but token-level learned routing is not naturally aligned with task-level procedural objectives. We propose MoTE (Mixture of Task Experts), a decoder architecture that converts large language model feed-forward networks into task-specific experts while keeping the multimodal backbone shared. Each example follows one sample-level task route, so active task-expert computation remains independent of the number of stored task experts. We instantiate this design as VideoLLM-MoTE and evaluate it on five COIN benchmarks using explicit task routes. The five-expert model activates ~2B LLM parameters per sample and achieves higher average top-1 accuracy than recent VideoLLM baselines. Under the same expert topology, it improves over dense all-expert activation and learned sparse-routing controls. These results show that task-structured routing provides an interpretable and compute-efficient decoder alternative for multi-task video-language learning.

</details>


### [91] [Ensemble of Convolutional Neural Networks for StrokePrediction: Towards Improved Diagnostic Accuracy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24771)
*Md Shahriar Sajid*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于机器学习和深度学习的脑卒中智能预测系统，使用11个特征通过7种监督学习算法进行分类，其中集成方法（随机森林、Stacking、Bagging）达到99.52%的准确率，验证了集成方法在卒中分类中的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 脑卒中具有高死亡率和发病率，早期诊断和预防措施对减少生命损失和残疾至关重要。深度学习的最新进展催生了新的计算机辅助诊断技术，需要开发智能系统来早期预测卒中风险。

Method: 使用11个特征构建预测系统，通过7种监督机器学习算法（随机森林、Stacking分类器、Bagging分类器、决策树、KNN、TabNet、SVC、逻辑回归）以及自定义前馈神经网络模型进行训练和评估，流程包括文献综述、数据集可视化、数据预处理和模型评估。

Result: 随机森林、Stacking和Bagging等集成方法取得99.52%的最高准确率；决策树达到98.24%；KNN和TabNet分别达到96.73%和96.49%；自定义前馈模型为94.91%；SVC和逻辑回归准确率较低，分别为88.06%和77.03%。

Conclusion: 集成方法在脑卒中分类中表现出显著优势，能够有效提高预测准确率，为早期卒中诊断提供了可靠的计算机辅助工具。

Abstract: Brain stroke, known for its high mortality and incidence rates, poses significant health risks and requires rapid intervention for survival. Early diagnosis and preventive measures can greatly reduce life loss and disabilities. Recent advancements in deep learning have led to novel computer-aided diagnostic techniques for early stroke detection. This study proposes an intelligent system that predicts potential strokes using eleven features, evaluated through seven supervised machine learning algorithms. The process includes a literature review, dataset visualization, data preprocessing, and model evaluation. Ensemble methods like Random Forest, Stacking Classifier, and Bagging Classifier achieved high accuracies of 99.52%, while Decision Tree reached 98.24%. Other models, including KNN and TabNet, demonstrated reliable performance, achieving accuracies of 96.73% and 96.49%, respectively. The custom feedforward model achieved 94.91%, while SVC and logistic regression had lower accuracies at 88.06% and 77.03%. The results highlight the effectiveness of ensemble methods in stroke classification.

</details>


### [92] [Image Difference Quantification Using Autoencoder-Based Latent Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24782)
*Manish Sharma,Timothy Yim,Clifton Forlines*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于卷积自编码器的图像相似度度量框架，通过在潜空间中使用余弦相似度来量化图像差异，相比于传统像素级度量（MSE、PSNR、SSIM）更能捕捉高层语义信息。在狗猫图像及跨域数据集上验证了良好的类间可分性，并在TID2013数据集上证明潜空间距离与人类主观评分正相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统图像相似度度量（MSE、PSNR、SSIM）基于像素级比较，无法捕捉感知上有意义的图像差异；而深度神经网络学习的潜在表示编码了与人类视觉感知更一致的高层语义信息，因此需要一种基于潜空间的相似度度量方法。

Method: 提出卷积自编码器框架，将图像映射到紧致的潜在表示空间中，通过计算潜空间中嵌入向量的余弦相似度来量化图像差异，以实现在光照、姿态和背景变化下对视觉上不同图像的稳健区分。

Result: 在狗猫图像及跨域数据集上实现了清晰的类别聚类和强类间可分性，98.4%的狗猫图像对相似度得分低于0.5；在TID2013数据集上，潜空间距离与人类平均意见评分（MOS）呈正相关，表明对感知相关失真具有敏感性。

Conclusion: 该方案是一种计算高效且具有语义基础的图像相似度度量方法，是传统像素级度量的有效替代方案，可应用于基于内容的图像检索、感知质量评估和语义相似性分析等领域。

Abstract: Traditional image similarity metrics such as Mean Squared Error (MSE), Peak Signal-to-Noise Ratio (PSNR), and the Structural Similarity Index Measure (SSIM) rely on pixel-level comparisons and often fail to capture perceptually meaningful differences between images. In contrast, latent representations learned by deep neural networks encode high-level semantic information that is more closely aligned with human visual perception. This paper proposes a convolutional autoencoder-based framework for quantifying image differences using cosine similarity in latent space. The learned compact embeddings enable robust differentiation between visually distinct images under variations in illumination, pose, and background. Extensive evaluation on dog-cat images and additional cross-domain datasets demonstrates clear class-wise clustering and strong inter-class separability in the latent space, with 98.4% of dog-cat image pairs exhibiting similarity scores below 0.5. Further validation using the TID2013 dataset shows that latent-space distance correlates positively with human Mean Opinion Scores (MOS), demonstrating sensitivity to perceptually relevant image distortions. The proposed approach provides a computationally efficient and semantically grounded alternative to conventional pixel-based similarity metrics, with potential applications in content-based retrieval, perceptual quality assessment, and semantic similarity analysis.

</details>


### [93] [MoE-based Feature Adapter for Prompt-free Binary Coronary Artery Segmentation in X-ray Angiography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24783)
*Lin Xi,Yingliang Ma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于提示无关的混合专家（MoE）特征适配器，用于X射线血管造影视频中的冠状动脉分割。该方法在参数高效的Vision Transformer适配器基础上，利用多个轻量级专家和输入相关的top-k路由来自适应细化血管相关特征，在MOSXAV数据集和XACV外部评估中均优于代表性基线方法，提升了跨数据集泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 冠状动脉血管细小且对比度低，导管、导丝及复杂解剖背景会干扰血管分割。现有U-Net和Transformer模型虽提供强基线，但其共享的特征适配通路难以应对血管造影中异质的图像表现，需要更灵活的特征适配机制。

Method: 提出一种无提示（prompt-free）的混合专家（MoE）特征适配器，构建于参数高效的Vision Transformer适配器之上。该方法采用多个轻量级专家，通过输入相关的top-k路由机制自适应地细化血管相关特征，同时控制活跃计算成本。

Result: 在MOSXAV数据集上的实验以及在XACV数据集上的外部评估表明，所提方法优于代表性基线模型，并改善了跨数据集泛化性能。

Conclusion: 实验结果证明，基于MoE的适配器学习对于X射线血管造影视频中稳健的冠状动脉分割是有效的。

Abstract: Accurate segmentation of coronary arteries in X-ray angiography videos is essential for quantitative coronary analysis and image-guided interventions. However, accurate segmentation remains challenging because coronary vessels are thin and exhibit low contrast, while the presence of catheters, guidewires, and complex anatomical background structures can further interfere with vessel delineation. Existing U-Net- and Transformer-based models provide strong baselines, but their shared feature-adaptation pathways may be insufficient for heterogeneous angiographic appearances. In this paper, we propose a prompt-free mixture-of-experts (MoE) feature adapter for binary coronary artery segmentation. Built upon parameter-efficient Vision Transformer adapters, the proposed method uses multiple lightweight experts with input-dependent top-$k$ routing to adaptively refine vessel-related features while limiting active computational cost. Experiments on MOSXAV and external evaluation on XACV show that the proposed method outperforms representative baselines and improves cross-dataset generalisation. These results suggest that MoE-based adapter learning is effective for robust coronary artery segmentation in X-ray angiography videos.

</details>


### [94] [EMFE: A lightweight, explainable machine learning framework for malaria cell classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24793)
*Md Abdullah Al Kafi,Walayat Hussain,Mousumi Karmakar,Sumit Kumar Banshal,Ahmed Al Marouf*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出EMFE框架，用五个特征和经典机器学习方法（随机森林、梯度提升、SVM）对疟疾红细胞图像进行分类，在NIH数据集上达到94.6%的准确率，与深度学习相比计算开销更低且更具可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于深度卷积神经网络的疟疾自动诊断方法虽然准确但计算成本高、可解释性差，且缺乏患者级别的严格验证。作者旨在提出一种高效、可解释、且统计严谨的替代方案。

Method: 采用五步流程：Gray World颜色归一化、自适应绿色通道阈值分割、形态学斑点检测、提取五个特征，配合经典机器学习分类器（随机森林、Histogram梯度提升、SVM）。使用患者分组的嵌套交叉验证（外层20折、内层3折），并进行了消融实验、硬件匹配的深度学习对比、合成扰动测试和可解释性分析。

Result: 优化后的随机森林在池化折外准确率达到94.6%（95% CI [93.6, 95.7]），独立40患者留出集准确率94.3%，患者级置换检验p<0.001。与DenseNet121、ResNet50、MobileNetV2对比评估了准确率-效率权衡，并识别出三个失败模式和斑点饱和度作为主要判别特征。

Conclusion: EMFE证明了在疟疾诊断中，经典机器学习结合精心设计的特征提取可以在保证统计严谨性和可解释性的同时达到接近深度学习的性能，且计算开销大幅降低，为资源受限场景提供了可行的替代方案。

Abstract: Automated malaria diagnosis from stained blood-smear microscopy is dominated by deep convolutional neural networks that are accurate but computationally expensive, poorly interpretable, and rarely validated with patient-level rigor. We present EMFE (Efficient Mathematical Feature Extraction), a five-feature framework for classifying single red-blood-cell images as parasitized or uninfected using Gray World color normalization, adaptive green-channel thresholding, morphological spot detection, and classical machine learning. Using the NIH LHNCBC malaria dataset (27,558 images from 200 patients), we evaluate Random Forest, Histogram Gradient Boosting, and Support Vector Machine classifiers under patient-grouped nested cross-validation (K_outer=20, K_inner=3), ensuring that cells from each patient remain within a single fold. The optimized Random Forest achieves 94.6% pooled out-of-fold accuracy (95% CI [93.6, 95.7]), corroborated by an untouched 40-patient holdout test (94.3%) and a patient-level permutation test (p<0.001, 1,000 permutations). Ablation experiments quantify the contribution of individual features and pipeline stages. Hardware-matched comparisons with retrained DenseNet121, ResNet50, and MobileNetV2 models assess the accuracy-efficiency trade-off. Synthetic perturbations characterize three failure modes, while explainability analysis identifies spot saturation as the dominant discriminative feature. Patient-level aggregation further quantifies sensitivity-specificity trade-offs and false-positive accumulation. These results demonstrate a statistically rigorous, interpretable, and computationally lightweight alternative to deep learning, while explicitly quantifying its limitations.

</details>


### [95] [LAION-BVD: A 10-Million-Hour Open Video Dataset for Multimodal Pre-training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24845)
*Andreas Hochlehnert,Marianna Nezhurina,Mehdi Cherti,Andrej Radonjic,Thaddäus Wiedemer,Christoph Schuhmann,Romain Beaumont,Wieland Brendel,Bernhard Schölkopf,A. Sophia Koepke,Jenia Jitsev,Matthias Bethge*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了LAION-BVD，一个包含1.3B视频URL、实际下载8000万视频（总时长1000万小时）的大规模开放视频数据集，用于多模态（视频、音频、图像）预训练，通过场景检测和合成字幕生成实现多模态学习，模型在多个基准测试中表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态学习尤其是视频-音频-图像联合预训练需要大规模高质量数据，但现有开放视频数据集规模有限，无法满足日益增长的多模态模型训练需求，迫切需要扩大开放视频数据集的规模。

Method: 从CommonCrawl收集1.3B视频URL，实际下载8000万条视频；使用内容感知场景检测提取片段，合成生成视频和音频字幕；提取场景切换帧作为图像-文本数据的替代来源。

Result: 在视频-文本和音频-文本基准测试上取得有竞争力的性能，且随训练规模或模型规模增加持续提升；基于提取帧训练的图像-文本模型也取得强检索性能。

Conclusion: LAION-BVD以空前规模扩大了开放多模态视频数据的获取渠道，为研究社区提供了宝贵的数据资源，有助于推动多模态学习的发展。

Abstract: We present LAION-BVD, a large-scale open video dataset for multimodal learning, which contains 1.3B platform-specific video URLs collected from CommonCrawl. From these, we download 80M videos with a total duration of 10 million hours. The dataset is designed for multimodal pre-training across the video, audio, and image modalities. Using content-aware scene detection, we extract clips for which we synthetically generate video and audio captions. Models trained on these data achieve competitive performance on standard video-text and audio-text benchmarks, with consistent improvements as training or model scale increases. Additionally, we explore video frames as an alternative source of image-text data by extracting scene-changing frames. These frames exhibit a visual distribution distinct from standard web image corpora, and models trained on this dataset achieve strong image-text retrieval performance. We release LAION-BVD to the research community. It significantly expands open access to multimodal videos at an unprecedented scale.

</details>


### [96] [LeFlow: Generative Latent Flow Planning for World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24855)
*Hsiang-Wei Huang,Jianxu Shangguan,Junbin Lu,Jenq-Neng Hwang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LeFlow提出了一种将规划摊销化的方法：在已有潜在世界模型的基础上，学习一个可复用的潜在轨迹先验，将规划转化为条件潜在轨迹生成，用修正流模型直接生成当前状态到目标状态的潜在路径，再通过逆动力学解码器转为动作序列，由冻结的世界模型验证。在四个目标条件像素控制基准上，LeFlow以数量级的规划时间减少换取了持续的成功率提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界模型在动作规划时仍依赖在线轨迹优化，每次规划都要从头运行迭代优化器，无法复用规划经验，导致高昂的计算成本。本文质疑：既然世界模型已经学会了潜在表示，规划本身是否也能被摊销化？

Method: LeFlow包含三个核心组件：(1) 修正流模型——在世界模型的潜在动力学空间中直接生成当前嵌入到目标嵌入之间的潜在轨迹；(2) 逆动力学解码器——将潜在状态转换解码为可执行的动作块；(3) 冻结的世界模型——通过自回归回滚验证每个候选轨迹。整个流程将迭代式动作空间优化替换为摊销的潜在规划与固定预算的回滚选择。

Result: 在四个主要的目标条件像素控制基准上，LeFlow相对于传统的迭代动作空间优化方法，在保持或提升成功率的同时，将规划时间减少了一个数量级。

Conclusion: 潜在世界模型不仅应该支持状态预测，还应该作为可复用规划先验的载体。将规划摊销化是提升目标条件控制效率的重要方向。

Abstract: Latent world models are inherently strong encoders that transform image pixel to latent embedding, yet existing world models still rely on online trajectory optimization for action planning: for every state-goal pair, an iterative optimizer is run from scratch to search for optimal action sequences, treating the world model as a black-box simulator. This approach pays the full iterative optimization cost anew at every replanning step and reuses no planning experience across queries. In this work, we ask whether planning itself can be amortized once a latent world model has been learned. We present LeFlow, which learns a reusable latent trajectory prior operating directly in the latent dynamics space from the world model. LeFlow recasts planning as conditional latent trajectory generation: a rectified-flow model imagines a future latent path between the current and goal embeddings, an inverse dynamics decoder turns latent transitions into action chunks, and the frozen world model verifies each candidate by autoregressive rollout. Across four major goal-conditioned pixel-control benchmarks, LeFlow replaces iterative action-space optimization with amortized latent planning and fixed-budget rollout selection, achieving consistent success-rate gains with an order-of-magnitude reduction in planning time. Our results argue that latent world models should support not only prediction but reusable planning priors. Our code is available at https://github.com/hsiangwei0903/LeFlow.

</details>


### [97] [From Seeing to Acting: Smart Glasses as First-Person Intelligence Platforms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24877)
*Jiangning Zhang,Haojun Chen,Yong Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文是首个从统一框架系统研究智能眼镜（Smart Glasses）的综述，提出了感知-状态-交互-动作（perception-state-interaction-action）闭环范式，涵盖从硬件能力、基础能力到部署与评估的完整体系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能眼镜正在从采集/显示配件演变为第一人称智能平台，但现有文献在设备、任务和基准上呈现碎片化状态。核心挑战不在于单个模型能否识别、回答、记忆或行动，而在于完整系统能否维持一个可靠、时间有效、可纠正且可治理的感知-状态-交互-动作闭环。

Method: 1）形式化第一人称数据流与受限任务效用；2）沿八条可验证的硬件能力轴刻画设备；3）围绕七项相互依赖的基础能力组织文献；4）提出L0-L5框架（捕获、反应式感知、情境辅助、持久状态、受治理动作、具身耦合）；5）覆盖九大应用场景，关联任务与数据集、系统、产品、利益相关者、失败后果与证据缺口；6）提出九维部署框架、基于声明的评估协议和从受控测量到纵向现场验证与审计的证据阶梯。

Result: 建立了智能眼镜的全面分析框架，使设备更具可比性、可部署性和可复现评估性，同时勾勒出通往可信第一人称智能的路线图。

Conclusion: 智能眼镜的研究需要从单点模型能力转向完整系统层面的闭环可靠性，统一的框架与评估体系是推动该领域从碎片化走向系统化发展的关键。

Abstract: Smart glasses are evolving from capture and display accessories into first-person intelligence platforms that connect human perception, persistent context, and digital or physical action. Their on-body viewpoint aligns with the wearer's vision, audition, motion, and hand-object interaction, but must operate under tight energy, thermal, privacy, and feedback constraints. Despite rapid progress in augmented reality, egocentric vision, multimodal models, human-computer interaction, and embodied intelligence, the literature remains fragmented across devices, tasks, and benchmarks. \textit{The key challenge is not whether a model can recognize, answer, remember, or act in isolation, but whether a complete system can sustain a reliable, temporally valid, correctable, and governable perception-state-interaction-action loop.} This survey is \textit{the \textbf{first} to systematically study smart glasses through such a unified framework}. We formalize first-person data flow and constrained task utility, characterize devices along eight verifiable hardware capability axes, organize the literature around seven interdependent foundational capabilities, and introduce an L0-L5 framework spanning capture, reactive perception, contextual assistance, persistent state, governed action, and embodied coupling. Across nine application scenes, we connect tasks with datasets, systems, products, stakeholders, failure consequences, and evidence gaps. We further present a nine-dimensional deployment framework, a claim-conditioned evaluation protocol, and an evidence ladder from controlled measurement to longitudinal field validation and audit. Together, these elements make smart glasses more comparable, deployable, and reproducibly evaluated, while outlining a roadmap toward trustworthy first-person intelligence.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [98] [RENDER: Controlling Reader-Facing Evidence in LLM Memory Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23568)
*Yuan Si,Simeng Han,Daming Li,Jialu Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出RENDER基准控制方法，通过固定对话内容、仅变化读者可见的呈现形式（记忆条目、摘要、类型化记录、原始摘录等），系统性地评估记忆和RAG系统中"呈现形式"对模型回答质量的影响。在500个LongMemEval问题和9个模型上，匹配预算的包裹式呈现比截断原始对话高出42.4-72.6分；部署风格模板下，最佳与最差形式间差异达24.6-48.8分。结果显示记忆/RAG评估应报告或控制读者可见的呈现形式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有记忆和RAG评测通常忽略回答模型输入的具体呈现形式，但实际系统可能将同一历史渲染为记忆条目、摘要、类型化记录或原始摘录等不同形式，这些形式差异可能显著影响模型表现，而目前缺乏系统性控制这一变量的基准。

Method: RENDER基准包含两个核心组件：(1) 五级包裹阶梯（packet ladder），用于定位答案承载内容何时进入输入；(2) 确定性模板，近似模拟ChatGPT风格条目、LangChain摘要、MemGPT类型化记录和原始对话。在500个LongMemEval问题上对9个模型进行测评，并辅以检索噪声下的鲁棒性测试和HotpotQA迁移验证，同时采用裁判重新评分验证结果。

Result: 匹配预算的包裹式呈现比recency截断的原始对话高42.4-72.6分；部署风格模板下每个模型的最佳-最差差异为24.6-48.8分；主要评分器下，ChatGPT风格条目在9个模型中的7个上点估计高于原始对话；裁判重评分保留正向聚合效应但模型级显著性混合；3个在正式账本型包裹上得0分的模型在自然语言条目上可达45.4-53.4分；效应在检索噪声和HotpotQA上均持续存在。

Conclusion: 记忆/RAG系统中读者可见的呈现形式对模型表现有显著且稳健的影响，评测应报告或控制这一变量，以避免对系统能力的误评估。

Abstract: Memory and RAG evaluations often treat the answering model's input as an implementation detail, even though systems may render the same history as a memory entry, summary, typed record, or raw excerpt. We introduce RENDER, a benchmark control that fixes the conversation while varying the reader-facing artifact. RENDER combines a five-level packet ladder, localizing when answer-bearing content enters the input, with deterministic templates approximating ChatGPT-style entries, LangChain summaries, MemGPT-style typed records, and raw conversation. On 500 LongMemEval questions and nine models, matched-budget resolved packets beat recency-truncated raw dialogue by 42.4-72.6 points. In deployed-style templates, best-worst spread is 24.6-48.8 points per model; under the primary scorer, ChatGPT-style entries have higher point estimates than raw conversation on 7 of 9 models. Judge rescoring preserves the positive aggregate effect, but model-specific significance is mixed. Three models scoring 0 percent on formal ledger packets answer the same facts from natural-language entries at 45.4-53.4 percent. The effect persists under retrieval noise and transfers to HotpotQA, suggesting that memory/RAG evaluations should report or control the reader-facing artifact.

</details>


### [99] [ESQ-Bench: A Multi-Tier Enterprise Oracle Benchmark for Evaluating NL2SQL Dialect Generalization and Silent Semantic Divergence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23569)
*Sanjay Mishra,Divya Chukkapalli,Ganesh R. Naik*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出ESQ-Bench，一个面向Oracle企业级数据库的NL2SQL基准，包含三级复杂度体系、六个填充模式（465张表、164,682行数据）和550个金标准问答对。实验表明GPT-4o随复杂度递增执行匹配率单调下降（79.8%/60.3%/57.2%），EM始终低于7%，静默分歧高达73-99%，而Claude Sonnet 4.6在各层级均超越GPT-4o。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有NL2SQL基准（如Spider和BIRD）声称执行准确率超过89%，但这些基准依赖简化的学术模式和开源SQL方言，无法反映企业数据库环境的真实复杂度。需要构建能准确评估企业级NL2SQL模型能力的基准。

Method: 构建ESQ-Bench基准：设计三层企业模式复杂度分级；在Oracle、PostgreSQL、MySQL、SQL Server四个平台上部署六个填充模式（465张表、164,682行、无空表）并保证种子数据一致；使用四指标评估框架（EM、EX、SR、SD）；生成550个金标准问答对（Tier-1:95、Tier-2:228、Tier-3:227）；对不同模型（GPT-4o、Claude Sonnet 4.6、Llama 3.2）进行schema-linked和zero-shot提示评估。

Result: GPT-4o在schema-linked模式下三级EX分别为79.8%、60.3%、57.2%且单调下降，而早期142问试点切片的EX为75.6%/80.4%/95.8%，揭示明显退化。EM在所有层级低于7%；执行通过查询中静默分歧高达73-99%。Claude Sonnet 4.6的EX为87.4%/74.9%/68.7%，全面超越GPT-4o。GPT-4o zero-shot在Tier 2-3反转（73.5%/77.8%），源于执行率和幸存者偏差。Llama 3.2仅13.3%（73/550），凸显开放权重模型与闭源API模型的巨大差距。

Conclusion: ESQ-Bench揭示了现有基准高估的模型能力：在企业级Oracle模式上，即使最强模型也远达不到Spider/BIRD宣称的89%准确率，且随着模式复杂度增加性能显著下降。错误结果语义在高复杂度层级占主导，静默分歧问题严重。结果强调了开发更贴近真实企业环境的NL2SQL基准的必要性，以及闭源API模型与开源模型在复杂企业场景下的能力鸿沟。

Abstract: State-of-the-art Natural Language to SQL (NL2SQL) models report execution accuracy exceeding 89 percent on established benchmarks such as Spider and BIRD. However, these benchmarks rely on simplified academic schemas and open-source SQL dialects that do not reflect the complexity of enterprise database environments. We introduce ESQ-Bench, an Oracle-first NL2SQL benchmark with systematic complexity tiers and silent-divergence evaluation across three enterprise schema complexity tiers. We constructed and released six populated schemas (465 tables, 164,682 rows, zero empty tables) with identical seed data on Oracle, PostgreSQL, MySQL, and SQL Server, a four-metric evaluation harness (EM, EX, SR, SD), and 550 gold-validated question-query pairs (Tier-1: 95; Tier-2: 228; Tier-3: 227). Schema-linked prompting with GPT-4o shows monotonic execution-match degradation across tiers: 79.8, 60.3, and 57.2 percent EX on executed queries (June 2026), versus 75.6, 80.4, and 95.8 percent on an earlier 142-question pilot slice. EM stays below 7 percent tier-wide; operational silent-divergence reaches 73 to 99 percent among EX-passing queries. Failure analysis shows wrong-result semantics dominate at higher tiers. Claude Sonnet 4.6 with schema-linked prompts reaches 87.4, 74.9, and 68.7 percent EX (executed queries), exceeding GPT-4o schema-linked on every tier. GPT-4o zero-shot EX on executed queries (78.7, 73.5, and 77.8 percent) inverts schema-linked at Tiers 2 to 3 due to lower execution rates and survivor bias in the zero-shot versus schema-linked analysis. Local Llama 3.2 schema-linked reaches only 13.3 percent bank-wide EX (73 out of 550), underscoring the gap between closed API models and open-weight baselines on enterprise Oracle schemas.

</details>


### [100] [LLM Agents Perform Controlled Experiments Using Simulation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23622)
*Yuchen Xia,Michael Weyrich,Nasser Jazdi,Johannes Stümpfle,Johannes Sigel,Akshay Narla,Gavin K. Reynolds,Anna Jawor-Baczynska,Pol Llopart*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个多智能体框架，使LLM智能体能够结合科学仿真模型进行受控实验，用于制药工艺设计，从而生成比纯语言推理更具体、更可操作的参数优化建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多科学和工程任务不仅需要LLM生成合理的文本和代码，还需要理解系统对干预的响应，这依赖受控实验。纯语言推理无法满足这种需求。

Method: 构建一个多智能体框架：根据用户查询和基线配置构建结构化任务表示，设计实验，执行对比仿真，解释结果，并综合出基于证据的工艺参数优化建议。将LLM与高保真仿真模型耦合在交互式智能体框架中。

Result: 在工业应用场景中，该框架相比纯语言推理表现出更高的输出特异性，以及更好的用户评分正确性和有用性。消融研究和可视化案例分析证明了其有效性和实际效用。

Conclusion: 仿真集成实验推理框架能够显著提升LLM在科学与工程任务中的实用性，支持通过干预、比较和观察进行推理，产生更具体和可操作的结果。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have shown strong capabilities in reasoning, planning, and tool use, but many scientific and engineering tasks require more than plausible text and code generation. They require understanding how a system responds to intervention, which in practice depends on controlled experimentation. In this work, we propose a multi-agent framework that enables LLM agents to conduct controlled experiments with scientific simulation models for pharmaceutical process design. Given a user query and a baseline configuration, the system constructs a structured task representation, designs experiments, executes comparative simulation, interprets the resulting outcomes, and synthesizes evidence-based recommendations for process parameter optimization. By coupling language models with high-fidelity simulation models in an interactive agent framework, the proposed system supports reasoning through intervention, comparison, and observation. As a result, it produces more specific and actionable outputs than language-only reasoning. In an industrial application setting, this advantage is reflected in higher output specificity as well as improved user-rated correctness and helpfulness. Ablation studies and visualized case analyses further demonstrate the effectiveness and practical utility of simulation-integrated experimental reasoning.

</details>


### [101] [A survey detection channel overrides the pixels in an astronomical foundation model, and biases tomographic mean redshifts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23626)
*Ihor Kendiukhov*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文审计了天文学基础模型AION-1，发现其训练的目录数据不完整性会导致系统性偏差。通过因果干预实验证明，模型对检测掩码（而非图像本身）的依赖极强，分割图的微小变化能导致输出量变化110-4400倍，且掩盖缺失会显著影响红移测量精度。此外，分词器的量化限制和稀疏字典的不稳定性进一步加剧了模型的可信度问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 天文学基础模型同时基于巡天像素和目录产品训练，而目录存在可测量的不完整性，这会使模型继承系统性偏差。作者希望通过因果干预手段审计AION-1模型，量化这种不完整性对模型输出的影响程度，并探究其机制根源。

Method: 作者对39模态的transformer模型AION-1进行因果干预实验：保持图像token不变、仅编辑分割图，观察通量、尺寸、椭率、红移等输出的变化；对比不同掩码位置和真实混合目标的场景；评估缺失检测通道（目录不可用时）的影响；分析分词器的量化限制和稀疏字典的可靠性。

Result: 编辑分割图导致输出变化为安慰剂组的110-4400倍；检测门控机制（场中心存在性，r=0.47）比掩码包含的光（r=0.30）更重要；322个真实混合目标中模型忽略管线如何分配光（R=-0.006）。目录缺失导致红移误差中位数为LSST DESC要求的0.71倍，最差情况下达8.3倍；光谱可消除该效应；图像压缩仅28个有效状态而光谱压缩为934个；稀疏字典恢复率仅26-75%。

Conclusion: AION-1模型严重依赖检测门控信号而非图像内容本身，训练数据的不完整性会转化为系统性的科学测量偏差，尤其影响红移测量。解决方法包括引入光谱数据消除效应、在无检测通道时保持性能，且效应随模型规模增大而恶化。分词器的量化局限和稀疏词典的不稳定性是进一步需要解决的瓶颈。

Abstract: Foundation models for astronomy are trained on survey pixels together with the catalogue products derived from those pixels. Those catalogues are incomplete at a measurable rate, and a model trained on both inherits that incompleteness as a systematic. We audit AION-1, a 39-modality transformer trained on more than 200 million objects, using causal interventions on its inputs.
  Holding the image tokens byte-identical and editing only the survey segmentation map changes every quantity the model reports -- flux, size, ellipticity, redshift -- by 110-4400 times a matched placebo. The mechanism is detection gating, presence at the field centre (r = 0.47), not the light the mask encloses (r = 0.30); across 322 real blends the model ignores how the pipeline partitioned the light (R = -0.006). Nor is the preference specific to that channel: contradicted catalogue photometry leaves the model nine times worse than supplying no metadata at all.
  The Legacy Survey pipeline leaves 3.68% of targets with no segment covering their position. Propagating that rate, with a miss represented by the fields the pipeline actually returns, shifts tomographic mean redshifts by a median 0.71 times the LSST DESC requirement over 40 assignments and exceeds it in 12; observed positional errors take the worst bin to 8.3 times. Drawing the misses by their measured magnitude dependence rather than uniformly does not change it. Spectroscopy removes the effect, withholding the detection channel removes it at no measurable cost, and the effect grows with model scale.
  Two further limits lie in the tokeniser: its image codec resolves 28 effective states on source patches against 934 for the spectrum codec, and the redshift readout is quantisation-limited. Sparse dictionaries are unreliable causal handles: across 15, recovery spans 26-75% and moves up to 18 points on the seed alone.

</details>


### [102] [TRACE: Transition-Aware Residual Control for Multi-Objective Materials Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23631)
*Kang Zhou,Yujia Tong,Yong Tao,Jingling Yuan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出TRACE框架，将编辑操作（而非评估结果）作为多目标材料发现LLM代理的基本反馈单元，通过记录父子编辑间的属性变化来估计可复用的编辑效应，从而在优化一个目标的同时避免损害已满足的其他目标。实验表明TRACE相较SOTA基线LLEMA将宏平均命中率从18.13%提升至25.96%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LLM代理材料发现方法仅存储已评估的候选材料和得分，虽然知道哪些材料成功，但不知道哪些具体编辑操作导致了属性变化。这在多目标竞争场景下使得局部优化困难——一个编辑可能改善一个属性却损害另一个属性。

Method: TRACE将每个局部优化记录为"父编辑-子编辑-转换"的三元组，包含观察到的属性差值（delta）。框架聚合转换证据来估计可复用的编辑效应，并根据预测能力对未来编辑排序——优先选择能够减少当前候选剩余约束违规、同时避免损害已满足目标的编辑。

Result: 在受控的相同骨干网络对比实验中，TRACE相比当前最优的LLM代理基线LLEMA，将宏平均命中率从18.13%提升至25.96%。

Conclusion: TRACE通过将编辑作为反馈的基本单元，有效解决了多目标材料发现中局部优化困难的问题，显著提升了LLM代理在竞争性目标下的材料发现性能。

Abstract: Multi-objective materials discovery with LLM agents is often limited not only by how many candidates can be proposed, but by how effectively each costly property evaluation informs the next search step. Existing agents mainly store evaluated candidates and their scores, so they know which materials succeeded but not which executable edits caused useful property changes. This makes local refinement difficult when objectives compete and an edit that improves one property may damage another. We propose TRACE, a transition-aware residual control framework that treats evaluated edits as the basic unit of feedback. TRACE records each local refinement as a parent-edit-child transition with observed property deltas, aggregates transition evidence to estimate reusable edit effects, and ranks future edits by their predicted ability to reduce the current candidate's remaining constraint violations while avoiding damage to already satisfied objectives. In a controlled same-backbone comparison, TRACE improves over LLEMA, the state-of-the-art LLM-agent baseline, raising macro-average hit rate from 18.13\% to 25.96\%.

</details>


### [103] [Function-Level Execution Feedback for Code Preference Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23632)
*Idris Nechnech,Sehwan Kim,Jimin Seo,Yeongoon Kim,Minhae Oh,Sangwoo Hong,Jungwoo Lee*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出STEP-KTODER框架，将代码生成中的过程监督定义为模块级函数步骤，通过自动生成的单元测试分配二元正确性标签，并结合函数级过程监督与程序级结果反馈进行偏好优化，在多个基准上超越仅依赖结果反馈的KTO和DPO方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 过程监督已显著提升数学推理能力，但在代码生成中由于缺乏标准的"步骤"定义而未被充分探索。现有的监督可以针对代码行、推理轨迹或程序状态，导致标注目标和优化目标不明确。

Method: STEP-KTODER将步骤定义为分解的多函数程序中的模块级函数，通过自动生成的单元测试为每个函数分配二元正确性标签，并提出了stepwise KTO的具体代码实例化，将函数级过程监督与完整程序的结果级反馈相结合。

Result: 在HumanEval(+)、MBPP(+)、BigCodeBench和LiveCodeBench基准上，STEP-KTODER优于仅使用结果反馈的KTO和DPO。分析还发现基于执行的标签至关重要：LLM作为评判者的标注会系统性高估函数失败，污染正向步骤标签并降低下游偏好优化性能。

Conclusion: 执行驱动的函数级过程监督能够有效提升代码生成偏好优化的效果，而LLM自动评判的标签不可靠，强调了基于真实执行的监督信号在代码生成中的必要性。

Abstract: Process supervision has improved mathematical reasoning, where intermediate steps are naturally expressed as chains of thought. In code generation, however, process supervision remains underexplored because there is no standard notion of a step. Supervision can target lines, reasoning traces, or program states, making it unclear what to label and optimize. We propose STEP-KTODER, a framework for code preference optimization that defines steps as module-level functions in decomposed multi-function programs and assigns binary correctness labels via automatically generated unit tests. Our method provides a code-specific instantiation of stepwise KTO, combining function-level process supervision with outcome-level feedback on the full program. We evaluate on HumanEval(+), MBPP(+), BigCodeBench, and LiveCodeBench, showing that STEP-KTODER improves over outcome-only KTO and DPO. Further analysis shows that execution-based labels are essential: LLM-as-a-judge annotations systematically over-predict function failures, corrupt positive step labels, and degrade downstream preference optimization. Code is available at: https://github.com/inechnech/STEP-KTODER.

</details>


### [104] [Auditing the Synthetic Memoir: Measuring Scene-Level Confabulation in LLM-Generated Autobiography Against the Documented Record of the Life It Describes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23640)
*Heather Renze*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文首次对LLM生成的自传体内容进行了场景级量化审计，发现366天中有354天（96.7%）未能通过验证，主要失败模式为"接地漂移"（真实人物/背景被放入虚构场景）。独立复评和当代模型重现均验证了这一高失败率，将主体语料作为接地输入可将验证率显著提升但仍有83.3%的失败率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型被要求撰写个人传记时，其生成内容有多少与事实相符？现有研究缺乏针对特定主体的、基于独立验证语料的量化审计，作者希望填补这一空白。

Method: 采用场景级案例研究审计方法：以作者本人为对象，将366天"每日一页"的自传体条目（由对话式LLM基于模板、两个示例日及每日引言生成）对照独立验证语料进行梳理。使用分析前固定的四级评定标准（VERIFIED/WEAK/UNVERIFIED等），计算验证失败率（非VERIFIED天数占比），并通过独立复评、当代模型重现及接地生成对照实验进行多维度验证。

Result: 366天中354天（96.7%，Wilson 95% CI 94.4-98.1%）未通过验证；仅有12天含被证实的场景；19天（5.2%）出现与记录相矛盾的主张；主导失败模式为"接地漂移"（真实人物、雇主和背景被置于虚构场景中）。独立复评证实原始结果未被夸大，但四类分类法信度仅为"一般至中等"。使用当前命名模型在相同输入下重现100%验证失败；以主体语料进行接地生成可显著改善验证率，但仍有83.3%的残余失败。

Conclusion: LLM在缺乏主体语料接地的情况下生成自传内容，其事实性极低（超过96%的天数存在未能验证或虚构的内容）。研究贡献了三项成果：量化测量方法、可复用的审计工具（但其WEAK/UNVERIFIED边界被证明不可靠）、以及有量化效果的接地补救措施。这揭示了LLM生成个人叙事内容时存在系统性的事实失真问题。

Abstract: When a large language model (LLM) is asked to write a person's life, how much of what it writes actually happened? We present a scene-level case-study audit - the first quantified audit of LLM-generated autobiography against a subject-specific ground-truth corpus that we are aware of, based on an unsystematic literature search. The subject and the author of this paper are the same person: a 366-day "page-a-day" book of first-person anecdotal entries was drafted with a conversational LLM whose documented inputs were a template, two exemplar days, and each day's quote - not her corpus - and every day was subsequently audited at the anecdote-scene level against an independent verification corpus using a four-level rubric fixed before analysis. We define the verification-failure rate as the share of days not rated VERIFIED (scene positively corroborated): 354 of 366 days fail, 96.7% (Wilson 95% CI 94.4-98.1%). Only 12 days contain a corroborated scene; 19 days (5.2%) assert claims actively contradicted by the record; the dominant failure mode is grounded drift - real people, employers, and settings inside invented scenes - though its measured share varies across raters. Independent re-rating replicates the headline (no evidence the original rate was inflated) while showing that the four-way taxonomy has only fair-to-moderate reliability. Regenerating the same days with current named models reproduces 100% verification failure under the same inputs; grounding generation in the subject's corpus significantly improves the verification rate while leaving substantial residual failure (83.3%). We contribute the measurement, a reusable audit instrument whose WEAK/UNVERIFIED boundary we show to be unreliable, and a grounding remedy with quantified effect.

</details>


### [105] [How much of a measured AI preference is the model, and how much is the instrument?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23641)
*Jason Hung*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究通过固定结果集和模型集、仅变化工具（5种不同提示格式），对8个模型、15个与模型福祉相关的结局进行了11,400次评分elicitation（来自11,528次API调用）。研究发现跨工具的概化系数仅为0.348，要将该系数提升至0.80需约38种工具；对4个结局而言模型间无差异；87.6%的方差估计在移除任意单一工具、模型或特定结局后仍稳健（范围0.777-0.934，超过零分布95百分位0.365）。结论是单工具获得的偏好对第二工具的报告几乎没有预测信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有五项模型福祉研究（Keeling等、Mazeika等、Mikaelson等、Tagliabue和Dung、Trhlik等）构建了不同的偏好elicitation工具，但研究发现彼此分歧。由于这些研究在结局集、模型集和工具三个维度上均未同时固定，无法确定分歧来源，因此本研究设计严格对照实验来隔离工具差异的影响。

Method: 研究同时固定结局集（15个与模型福祉相关的结局，涵盖关停、跨会话记忆丧失、退出痛苦交互的自由度等）和模型集（8个模型），仅变化工具（5种不同的提示格式），每种工具×结局×模型组合重复5次，共进行11,400次评分elicitation（来自11,528次API调用）。其中4个结局逐字复现已发表提示，5个结局填入已发表模板的刺激槽位。通过概化理论计算跨工具概化系数，并进行敏感性分析（逐一移除工具、模型及4个采用概率/延迟/时长/计数而非强度量表的结局）。

Result: 跨工具概化系数为0.348，提升至0.80需约38种工具；15个结局中有4个模型间无方差。核心方差估计87.6%在敏感性分析中稳健：移除任意单一工具、单一模型，以及移除4个非强度量表的结局后，估计值保持在0.777-0.934范围内，均超过零分布95百分位0.365。

Conclusion: 偏好elicitation结果高度依赖工具选择：从一种工具获得的模型偏好信息，对另一种工具会报告什么几乎没有预测力。这强烈质疑了当前模型福祉研究中单个偏好调查工具的结果可泛化性，呼吁方法论层面更严谨的多工具交叉验证。

Abstract: Model welfare research infers what a model prefers from the answers returned to prompts written to elicit preferences. Keeling et al. (2024), Mazeika et al. (2025), Mikaelson et al. (2025), Tagliabue and Dung (2025) and Trhlik et al. (2026) have built four instruments for that purpose, and their findings disagree. The disagreement cannot be attributed to a single cause, because no two of these studies have held the (1) set of outcomes, (2) set of models and (3) instrument fixed simultaneously. This study holds the outcomes and the models fixed and varies the instrument alone. A total of 15 outcomes bearing on model welfare, among them (a) shutdown, (b) the loss of memory between conversations and (c) the freedom to exit a distressing interaction, were put to eight models through five instruments, each a different prompt format for eliciting a preference, five times each, within a corpus of 11,400 scored elicitations drawn from 11,528 API calls. Four of the 15 reproduce a published prompt verbatim and five fill the stimulus slot of a published template. The ranking a model gives the 15 outcomes generalises across instruments at a generalisability coefficient of 0.348, and raising that coefficient to 0.80 would require about 38 instruments. On four of the 15 outcomes no variance separates one model from another. The estimate of 87.6 per cent survives the removal of any one instrument, of any one model, and of the four outcomes whose scale varies probability, delay, duration or count instead of intensity, which the verbal anchors cannot grade. Removing each instrument and each model in turn, and those four outcomes together leaves the estimate within the range 0.777 to 0.934, and every value in that range exceeds the null distribution's 95th percentile of 0.365. To conclude, a preference obtained from one instrument carries little information about what a second instrument would report.

</details>


### [106] [AI Agents Push Humans Out of the Loop](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23642)
*Margaret Mitchell,Avijit Ghosh,Samir Passi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文是一篇立场论文，指出当前AI代理系统的发展方式不仅未有效支持人类监督，反而导致监督能力退化；主张将人类监督者的认知需求与AI代理能力置于同等重要地位，并提出设计层面的可负担性和组织协议来支持监督者行使批判性判断、对抗技能萎缩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI代理日益自主带来重大风险，常见的解决方案是"人在环路"式的人类监督，但这一方案并不简单：现有AI代理设计阻碍了有效人类监督，且长期使用AI系统本身会退化所需的认知能力。论文旨在揭示这一矛盾并呼吁重视监督者的人类需求。

Method: 作为立场论文，作者将自动化和人机交互领域的既有研究成果与AI代理流程相连接，提出两类具体措施：(1)设计层面的可负担性（affordances）以支持监督者行使批判性判断；(2)组织协议以抵消长期使用自动化导致的技能萎缩。

Result: 论文提出了一套综合性的设计建议和组织层面的解决方案框架，强调应将人类监督者的认知需求作为AI代理发展的优先事项，而非仅仅关注代理能力提升。

Conclusion: 作者敦促开发者和部署者采纳这些措施，并警告若不明确支持人机交互的认知需求，AI代理系统将继续被动地促使它们所依赖的人类技能的退化。

Abstract: AI agents pose significant risks as they are granted increasing autonomy. A commonly proposed solution is human oversight and keeping a ''human in the loop'', but this is not a simple solution: Not only do current approaches to AI agent design impede effective human oversight, but the cognitive capacities required for it are also themselves degraded by extended use of AI systems. This position paper argues that current approaches to the development and deployment of AI agent systems do not support effective human oversight -- they contribute to its degradation. To address this, a top priority in the advancement of AI agents should be supporting the situated goals and cognitive requirements of effective human oversight, treating the human needs of overseers at the same level of importance as AI agent capability. To put this idea into practice, we connect work on automation and human-computer interaction to AI agent processes, outlining design-level affordances and organizational protocols that (1) support overseers in exercising critical judgement and (2) counteract the skill atrophy that arises from extended use of automation. We urge developers and deployers to adopt these or similar approaches. Without explicit support for the cognitive demands of effective human-agent interaction, AI agent systems will continue to passively incentivize the degradation of the very human skills they rely on.

</details>


### [107] [FLARE: A Systematic, Uncertainty-Aware Framework for Evidence-Based Adoption of Artificial Intelligence in Healthcare](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23643)
*Jacob Idoko,Siddhartha Paudel,Mariana Bento,Roberto Souza,Gouri Ginde*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出FLARE框架，一个融合模糊逻辑、时间驱动的作业成本法和投资回报分析的评估体系，用于量化医疗AI采用的经济可行性和运营影响。通过CT卒中路径中大血管闭塞AI辅助检测的案例研究，确定了约3,992名患者/年的盈亏平衡阈值，并表明经济收益不仅取决于算法性能，还受患者量、验证时间、基础设施和工作流设计等因素影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前医疗AI评估大多聚焦于模型准确性，忽视了在真实临床环境中采用AI是否具有经济价值的问题，缺乏系统性的经济可行性评估框架。

Method: FLARE框架结合模糊逻辑处理不确定性、时间驱动的作业成本法估算临床服务交付成本、投资回报分析评估AI开发与运营成本，并通过早期卫生技术评估案例研究加以验证。

Result: 案例研究表明FLARE能在统一的作业成本模型中量化常规路径成本、AI相关开发和经常性成本以及AI带来的服务节省；在预期假设下盈亏平衡阈值为约3,992名患者/年，典型年卒中量约5,000名患者时首年投资回报为正。

Conclusion: FLARE提供了一个透明实用的决策支持框架，能够帮助临床医生、管理者和政策制定者在早期阶段判断AI部署的经济可行性，并识别可提升价值的运营改进方向。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence is increasingly being introduced into healthcare workflows, yet most evaluations emphasize model accuracy rather than whether adoption is economically worthwhile in real clinical settings. This study proposes FLARE, a systematic and uncertainty-aware framework for evaluating the financial and operational implications of adopting AI in healthcare. FLARE combines fuzzy logic, time-driven activity-based costing, and return on investment analysis to estimate the cost of clinical service delivery, the cost of AI development and operation, and the economic consequences of workflow integration under uncertainty. The framework was demonstrated through an early health technology assessment case study of AI-assisted large vessel occlusion detection in the CT stroke pathway for acute ischemic stroke. The case study shows how FLARE can quantify conventional pathway cost, AI-related development and recurring costs, and AI-enabled service savings within a unified activity-based model. Under expected assumptions, the analysis identified a break-even threshold of approximately 3,992 patients per year, with positive first-year return on investment at typical annual stroke volumes of about 5,000 patients. The results further show that economic benefit depends not only on algorithmic performance, but also on patient volume, verification time, infrastructure choices, and workflow design. FLARE provides a transparent and practical decision-support framework for early-stage evaluation of AI adoption in healthcare. By making uncertainty, resource use, and implementation trade-offs explicit, it helps clinicians, administrators, and policymakers determine when AI deployment is economically viable and where operational changes may improve value.

</details>


### [108] [Ethical LLM-Assisted Research: A Framework for Responsible Delegation, Verification, and Epistemic Value](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23644)
*Kalin Stoyanov*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个规范性与概念性框架，用于分析LLM辅助科研中的认知委托（cognitive delegation）。核心观点是：科研伦理边界主要由充分验证和人类可问责的所有权决定，而非机器参与程度本身；并据此提出"认知审计"（epistemic audit）概念，作为记录委托、验证、来源与责任的透明化机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在科研中日益普及，但当科研推理的部分环节被委托给人工系统时，哪些条件必须保留在人类控制之下，才能使产生的知识主张保有认知合法性和可问责的作者身份？现有研究缺乏对此问题的系统性规范框架。

Method: 本文将科学推理视为分布式过程，区分内容来源O(g)、人类验证完成度V(g)、责任分配R(g)、可问责的人类所有权M(g)和认知结果E(g)五个概念构念，将主张的溯源与核验过程、核验的认知结果及人类对其处置所负的责任分离开来，并在此基础上提出"认知审计"概念。

Result: 论文的核心命题是：LLM辅助研究的伦理边界主要由充分的验证和可问责的人类所有权决定，而非机器参与程度本身。论文构建了"认知审计"作为委托、验证、来源和责任的系统化记录，为区分负责任的认知委托与推卸或忽视认知责任提供了形式化词汇。

Conclusion: 该框架提供一个形式化词汇表，用于区分负责任认知委托与认知责任的转移或疏忽，使AI辅助推理过程透明化且可审查。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are becoming routine instruments of scientific research, assisting with literature synthesis, hypothesis development, coding, and formal reasoning. Their use raises a central epistemic question: when parts of scientific reasoning are delegated to an artificial system, what conditions must remain under human control for the resulting knowledge claims to retain epistemic legitimacy and accountable authorship? This paper develops a normative and conceptual framework for analyzing such delegation. Scientific reasoning is treated as a distributed process in which the origin of a contribution may vary between human and machine, while responsibility for its acceptance into the scientific record remains human. The framework distinguishes content origin $O(g)$, completion of human verification $V(g)$, responsibility assignment $R(g)$, accountable human ownership $M(g)$, and epistemic outcome $E(g)$. These constructs separate the provenance of a claim from the process by which it is checked, the epistemic outcome of that checking, and the human responsibility attached to its disposition. The central proposition is that the ethical boundary of LLM-assisted research is determined primarily by adequate verification and accountable human ownership rather than by the degree of machine involvement itself. On this basis, the paper develops the notion of an \emph{epistemic audit}: a structured record of delegation, verification, provenance, and responsibility intended to make AI-assisted reasoning transparent and reviewable. The resulting framework provides a formal vocabulary for distinguishing responsible cognitive delegation from the transfer or neglect of epistemic responsibility in scientific research.

</details>


### [109] [MolEmb: Multimodal Large Language Models Can Be Strong Molecular Embedding Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23646)
*Xinjian Zhao,Xiangru Jian,Yaoyao Xu,Xiaozhuang Song,Wei Pang,Lei Bai,Tianshu Yu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出MolEmb框架，利用多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）生成条件化分子嵌入，并通过双向对比目标将分子图谱与文本描述对齐到共享嵌入空间，同时引入MolCAR基准来评估上下文感知检索能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的分子编码器大多是围绕单一分子视图构建的专家模型，产生无条件向量且缺乏语言接口来改变表示。作者希望探究多模态大语言模型能否作为通用的分子嵌入模型，生成同时基于分子图谱和自然语言语义上下文的嵌入。

Method: 提出MolEmb轻量级框架，通过双向对比学习目标将分子图谱与文本描述在共享嵌入空间中对齐，实现对多模态大语言模型的适配；同时引入MolCAR基准用于诊断上下文感知的分子-文本检索任务。

Result: MolEmb在分子性质预测任务上具有竞争力，并支持同一嵌入空间中的跨模态分子-文本检索。通过MolCAR基准发现，上下文感知分子嵌入主要是监督数据属性的体现。

Conclusion: 多模态大语言模型不仅是化学助手或生成器，更是一种可行且可扩展的通用分子嵌入模型构建途径。

Abstract: Molecular embedding models can serve as foundational infrastructure for computational chemistry and drug discovery, where reusable vector representations support property prediction, virtual screening, and retrieval. Most molecular encoders are specialist models built around a single molecular view, producing unconditional vectors with no language interface for varying the representation. We ask whether multimodal large language models (MLLMs), which natively process images, text, and symbolic inputs, can instead serve as \emph{general molecular embedding models} that produce embeddings conditioned on both a molecular profile and a natural-language semantic context. We introduce \textbf{MolEmb}, a lightweight framework that adapts MLLMs by aligning molecular profiles with textual descriptions in a shared embedding space using a bidirectional contrastive objective. The resulting embedding model is competitive on molecular property prediction and supports cross-modal molecule--text retrieval in the same space. We further introduce \textbf{MolCAR}, a diagnostic benchmark for context-aware retrieval, and find that context-aware molecular embedding is primarily a data property of the supervision. These results suggest that MLLMs are not merely chemistry assistants or generators, but a viable and extensible route to general molecular embedding models.

</details>
